
====================================================================================================
MONEYBOARD 2026 | COMPLETE APP AUDIT, COMPETITOR REVIEW ANALYSIS, ASO, LAUNCH AND GROWTH PLAN
====================================================================================================

DATE: 2026-10-30 | REGION: India | WORKSPACE: P:\Projects\MoneyBoard-master
SOURCES: direct repository reads, saved public Google Play samples, live Google policy research.
EVIDENCE LABELS: [CODE] verified in repository; [TEST] actually executed; [PLAY] public listing/reviews;
[POLICY] official Google Play documentation; [PROPOSED] work not implemented; [OPINION] analyst scoring.
EXECUTIVE VERDICT: Strong idea for Indian salaried users and an attractive visual direction, but NOT
READY TO SHIP. A current Gradle run fails to compile. Production startup inserts a fictional Rs 70,000
salary plan, the backup is incomplete and has no demonstrated restore, actual income can silently display
planned income, and some financial comparison claims have no supporting data. Fix these before marketing,
monetization or release.

REVIEW RESEARCH: 14 competing apps; 1,375 records in first scrape + 909 in second = 2,284 sampled observations;
after exact package+review-ID deduplication: 1,591 DISTINCT public review records. No fabricated reviews.

====================================================================================================
01 | CURRENT CONDITION AND 0-10 SCORECARD
====================================================================================================

Score legend: 0-2 absent/blocking, 3-4 weak, 5-6 plausible prototype, 7-8 strong foundation, 9-10 market-
tested and reliable. Ratings are subjective opinions based on one screenshot, inspected source and
compilation output; not user-survey results.

Visual identity / design          7.0/10 | weight  8 | Well-crafted blue poster identity; dense and truncated category labels.
First-run usability               4.0/10 | weight  9 | 24 preset categories and fictional salary obstruct honest onboarding.
Core functionality                6.0/10 | weight  8 | Plan/Actual, editing, category management, transaction entry and exports exist.
Financial math / accuracy         3.0/10 | weight 12 | Actual income uses plan when no real income was recorded.
Build and reliability             2.0/10 | weight 14 | Current Kotlin compilation fails; tests cannot run.
Backup and recovery               1.5/10 | weight 14 | One-month JSON summary is not a full user data backup.
Local-first privacy posture       7.0/10 | weight  6 | Room and no network permission in source manifest; SDK audit outstanding.
Accessibility                     4.0/10 | weight  5 | Small-screen, text scale and TalkBack checks not verified.
India-specific relevance          7.5/10 | weight  5 | Rent, SIP, LIC, family support and local budget categories.
Market opportunity                6.5/10 | weight  4 | Demonstrated demand, but crowded with large incumbents.
Differentiation                   5.0/10 | weight  5 | Salary allocation angle meaningful; similar competing planners exist.
Store ASO and identity            3.0/10 | weight  4 | Uncleared brand collision with an existing MoneyBoard app.
Retention mechanics               3.5/10 | weight  3 | Manual logging possible; durable habit loop unproven.
Monetization readiness            2.5/10 | weight  2 | No verified billing tests or subscriber demand.
Production release readiness      1.0/10 | weight  7 | Compile blocker plus data loss and Play compliance gaps.
CURRENT WEIGHTED READINESS: 3.87/10. RELEASE DECISION: NO-GO, until all P0 fixed and a signed release tested.

====================================================================================================
02 | REPOSITORY VERIFICATION: CURRENT CAPABILITIES
====================================================================================================

- [CODE] app/build.gradle.kts: Kotlin/Compose Material3, Room 3, minSdk 26, targetSdk 37, versionName
  0.3.0, versionCode 1, package com.premraj.moneyboard.
- [CODE] core/database, core/data, DashboardViewModel: Room stores categories, accounts, plans and
  transactions; month navigation and StateFlow-backed dashboard.
- [CODE] MoneyBoardApp.kt: manual transaction logging, edit/archive/restore category, plan vs actual
  switching, setting salary, report sharing, CSV/PDF/PNG and JSON snapshot export.
- [CODE] AndroidManifest.xml: android:allowBackup=false; FileProvider for share; source manifest has no
  INTERNET permission. Local-first is defensible, 'zero data collected' still needs SDK review.
- [CODE] UserSettings.kt defaults Pune, Single Person and Rs 70,000; MoneyBoardApplication.kt always
  seeds a demo monthly income plan on first empty month in release too.
- [CODE] One reviewed screenshot is visually cohesive but uses a dense two-column budget list;
  accessibility and small-screen behavior cannot be certified from screenshot.
- [HISTORICAL] TEST_READY.md says 59 tests passed Oct 28; these are old results, superseded by Oct 30
  compile failure.
- [NOT VERIFIED] Full backup restore, scheduled recurring bills, cross-device family sync, account login,
  accurate analytics, production adoption or Play listing are not established.


====================================================================================================
03 | EXACT TECHNICAL BLOCKERS AND REQUIRED ACCEPTANCE TESTS
====================================================================================================

P0-A current build broken
EVIDENCE: DashboardUiModels.kt:88,90 calls Money.inrMinor(...) but Money.kt has no such companion
function. ExpenseReportSheet.kt:199-204 passes incorrect ReportBottomRow parameter names.

DONE WHEN: Correct call and signature semantics. Rerun assembleDebug, unit tests, lint, signed release
build and export render checks.

P0-B fabricated user money
EVIDENCE: MoneyBoardApplication.kt calls ReferenceDemoPlanSeeder.seedIfEmpty(YearMonth.now()) in
production, persisting Rs 70,000 salary and 24 category amounts into Room.

DONE WHEN: Production never seeds actual user financial values; add optional sample mode with unambiguous
SAMPLE label and safe data migrations.

P0-C incomplete backups / possible unrecoverable data
EVIDENCE: DataBackupHelper.kt serializes one month settings, expenseRows and summary, not transaction
database, all prior months or account history. No full restore flow identified; manifest backup off.

DONE WHEN: Versioned all-data JSON/zip plus transactional restore onto fresh phone; compare record IDs,
months and totals before/after.

P0-D misleading plan/actual
EVIDENCE: DashboardCalculator.kt sets effectiveActualIncome = actualIncomeRaw if positive else
plannedIncome; actual dashboard then uses planned income as if received.

DONE WHEN: Zero actual INCOME must show zero or clearly labeled forecast, with tests for absent, positive
and reversed income.

P0-E unjustified comparisons
EVIDENCE: FinancialHealthCard.kt claims user beats 85% of urban professionals without cited dataset.
CompetitorComparisonDialog includes claims about competitors and no battery drain that are not verified.

DONE WHEN: Remove unsupported comparative statements; use only verifiable local numbers and measured
evidence.

P1-F mobile readability
EVIDENCE: FinanceBoardScreen and supplied screenshot show many items in two columns and truncated labels.

DONE WHEN: 320dp / 360dp / large-text, keyboard inset, high-contrast, screen-reader and one-hand tasks
pass.

P1-G export correctness
EVIDENCE: Report export sheet contains mismatched parameters at compile time; separate plan and actual
inputs must be tested end to end.

DONE WHEN: CSV/PDF/PNG totals agree with Room for selected month to 0.01 INR, including
zero/negative/large values.


====================================================================================================
04 | PRODUCT-MARKET FIT, IDEAL BUYER AND POSITIONING
====================================================================================================

- Primary: salaried Indian 18+ household planner who wants salary -> fixed obligations -> variable spend
  -> savings -> remaining money, with no bank connection.
- Secondary: new earner or family budget maker who needs control of monthly categories and a simple real-
  transaction log; allow empty income and a small category preset list.
- Future: two adults managing shared household records, only once secure invitation, sync, role
  permissions and conflict resolution exist.
- Do not attempt v1 replacement for bank account aggregators, brokerage, credit platforms or professional
  accounting suites.
- Positioning: 'Plan where your salary goes. Track what you actually spent. Know what's left.' This is a
  user promise to build and test, not a guarantee of financial outcomes.
- Competitive wedge: monthly salary allocation in INR + flexible India-specific household categories +
  verified offline operation + genuinely recoverable user-owned data + exportable report.
- Must-meet bar: Cashew-level modern UI, Monefy-level quick capture, Realbyte/AndroMoney reliability,
  Wallet planning depth and a simpler onboarding path.

====================================================================================================
06 | ALL REVIEW STARS, AGGREGATE THEMES AND INTERPRETATION
====================================================================================================

METHOD: An earlier run collected newest 25 reviews separately for each star 1-5 across 11 apps; a later
run collected 20 reviews per star from 10 apps. Union has 14 apps and 1,591 unique review IDs. Family
Wallet returned only nine 5-star reviews. This is NONRANDOM, star-balanced convenience sampling for
understanding types of issues. It absolutely cannot estimate star proportions, Indian market share,
satisfaction rates or conversion. Review country is not guaranteed by use of an 'in' Play query. Keyword
tags are automated, imperfect and can attach to positive or negative mentions; non-English content can be
underclassified. A 5-star review may describe a bug. Long third-party review text is not reprinted;
analysis and metadata are paraphrased or indexed.

UNIQUE REVIEW COUNTS: 1 star=316, 2 star=315, 3 star=315, 4 star=316, 5 star=329; total 1591.

====================================================================================================
08 | PRIORITIZED FEATURE OPPORTUNITY LIST FROM 1,591 REVIEWS
====================================================================================================

- P0. Real, reproducible, ALL-HISTORY backup and import/restore after changing phones. Test integrity;
  data belongs to users, no false confidence from a partial 'backup'.
- P0. Clear planned salary vs real received income, planned vs spent amounts, savings vs expense and
  budget remainder, all mathematically consistent.
- P1. One-hand quick entry (target <10 seconds for familiar category), last-used category, transaction
  history search, edit and undo delete.
- P1. Five-minute maximum setup, optionally fewer default categories, salary skip, customizable labels
  and groups, helpful empty state.
- P1. Smooth modern UI with reduced animation option, readable font scaling, 48dp targets and no category
  truncation that hides meaning.
- P1. India-first INR formatting, SIP, LIC, EMI, family support, rent and annual funds as optional
  categories; transparent difference between saved money and spending.
- P1. CSV/PDF/PNG exports that match exactly; explain that reports differ from complete archives. Safe
  file share and personal-data disclosure.
- P2. Recurring bill and payday reminders, scheduled transactions, optional on-device notifications with
  proper permission and opt-out.
- P2. Custom category reorder, preset templates, month-over-month trend and spending pace; calculations
  should be explainable.
- P2. Clear paid/free boundary if adding subscriptions or one-time purchases; never put restore behind a
  paywall.
- P3. Shared family budgets ONLY with secure authorization, roles, conflict-safe sync and deletion
  pathways; cannot claim this now.
- P3. Optional automated import, SMS or bank connection only if demand outweighs privacy, permission and
  support costs; do not build to match competitor checkboxes.


====================================================================================================
09 | DETAILED IDEAL APPLICATION PRODUCT SPECIFICATION
====================================================================================================

First launch / onboarding
Three steps: explain private manual budgeting and how Plan differs from Actual; enter actual salary or
skip; choose only the relevant categories. A sample demo is opt-in and watermarked SAMPLE, isolated from
financial records. No forced INR 70,000 plan.

Home dashboard
Month/year navigation, clear Plan vs Actual segmented switch, salary planned/received as separate labeled
values, committed allocation, actual spending, savings, and distinct 'left to allocate' and 'available
after spending'. Primary Add Transaction action visible above the fold.

Category planning
Use collapsible list rather than 24 always-visible cards. Search, filter, re-order categories.
Planned/used/left in one row, readable on 320dp phones. Edit or archive without corrupting past months.
Negative/over-budget values visible with text and accessible color semantics.

Transaction capture
Types Income/Expense/Saving/Investment, amount in minor units, date, account, category, optional note;
add/edit/undo; sensible defaults without assuming account balance. Transfers and refunds need exact
accounting semantics before exposure.

Reports
Month-scoped Plan vs Actual, category shares, savings, exports; all read from same canonical snapshot.
Poster graphic is an optional beautiful extra, not the main way to check daily balances. Actual vs Plan
color mapping consistent.

Backup and restore
Dedicated screen explaining full history coverage, preview counts, encrypted or protected user-selected
storage as available, import and integrity report. Explicitly distinguish this from a one-month exported
summary.

Settings / support
Theme, text scaling, currency if implemented, default dashboard mode, true privacy statement, help and
support, export/restore controls, clear all local records with warning and optional backup first.

Future household sharing
Invite another member, consent and permissions, conflict resolution, offline edits reconciliation, role-
based deletion; a distinct roadmap item after launch and not in store copy until verified.


====================================================================================================
10 | ENGINEERING ARCHITECTURE, INVARIANTS AND PROOF CHECKLIST
====================================================================================================

- Keep domain Money integer minor units; use exact math and validated ISO 4217 currency, never silently
  join unlike currencies or truncate paise.
- Explicitly distinguish ACTUAL received income from PLANNED income; show zero/no-record state when user
  has not logged salary. Forecast uses a separate label and value.
- Design transaction state with stable ID, date, type, category, amountMinor, currency, account, note and
  deletion metadata; define one sign convention and avoid double counts.
- Versioned backup: schemaVersion, exportTimestamp, settings, all accounts, all categories, income plans,
  category plans, all transactions, currencies and tombstones across every month. Preserve unknown future
  fields or reject safely.
- Restore: user picks file -> parse/validate -> preview totals and counts -> safeguard existing data ->
  Room transaction insert/replace/merge -> duplicate policy -> verify checksum/record totals -> show
  completion. Corrupt file must never destroy the current DB.
- Use Android Storage Access Framework to place a user-owned persistent backup, rather than relying on
  cacheDir (which the system may clear). Consider documented optional Android backup policy only after
  designing security.
- Test account changes, category archive, month transitions, negative and very large values, currency
  accuracy, concurrent edits, export under cancellation and process death.
- No bank-grade or guaranteed encryption claim until threat model and implementation are audited. Inspect
  merged manifest and resolved third-party SDKs before Google Play Data safety answers.
- Privacy-sensitive data must not appear in logs, screenshots made automatically, crash reporting, or
  shared temporary files without user intent.
- Tests: ./gradlew.bat assembleDebug testDebugUnitTest lintDebug bundleRelease (or explicit release
  build) and instrumented database migration/restore tests on real hardware.
- Play safety: release signing key stored securely, signed AAB installed/tested via Play internal track,
  app upgrade migrations and older supported APIs checked.


====================================================================================================
11 | ASO: NAME, PACKAGE NAME, KEYWORDS AND BRAND CLEARANCE
====================================================================================================

BRAND FINDING: An existing Android family/household budget app by Yusuke Saso already uses 'MoneyBoard -
Budget Blanner' (package com.lancerdog.money_board, 10,000+ installs in a third-party Play data listing).
An older, unrelated 'Moneyboard' keyboard app also previously used that brand. A generic MoneyBoard title
risks collisions. 'Salary Flow - Budget Planner', 'RupeeFlow' and 'Monthly Salary Planner' also have
current Play listings. Search is not trademark or app-ID availability clearance; perform
legal/domain/store checks before publication.

PRIMARY WORKING BRAND: SalaryLane
PLAY TITLE: SalaryLane: Budget Planner (26 of max 30 characters)
NEW APPLICATION ID IF NOT YET RELEASED: com.premraj.salarylane
CURRENT CODE APPLICATION ID: com.premraj.moneyboard
ALTERNATIVE TITLES, ALL REQUIRE INDEPENDENT CLEARANCE:
   27/30  RupeeRoster: Salary Planner
   28/30  PaydayCanvas: Budget Planner
   27/30  SalaryPath: Expense Planner
   25/30  MoneyBoard: Salary Budget
   26/30  SalaryLane: Budget Planner
Name logic: Salary communicates the primary intent; Budget Planner communicates the job of the app; a
coined prefix may improve recall. In Google Play, package IDs identify the app and are effectively
immutable after release. Package name words are not a substitute for title and description ASO. If
com.premraj.moneyboard is already published, preserve the ID for updates unless launching a different
application. Confirm package uniqueness and selected name with Play Console and trademark search; web
search alone cannot certify either.

PRIMARY QUALITATIVE KEYWORD CLUSTERS (NOT MEASURED VOLUMES): salary budget planner; monthly budget planner; expense tracker; budget tracker; income expense tracker; money manager.
SECONDARY RELEVANCE: INR money planner; monthly salary allocation; household expenses; family budget; EMI and bill budget; savings planner; report export.
KEYWORDS TO AVOID BEFORE FEATURES EXIST: bank sync, AI finance advisor, automatic SMS tracking, cross-device family sync, encrypted cloud backup.
TEST: compare salary-first vs family-first Play listing experiments, control audience/creative, track listing-to-install conversion and first budget completion; do not fabricate keyword scores.

14 | GOOGLE PLAY POLICY / COMPLIANCE REQUIREMENTS AS OF 2026-10-30
====================================================================================================

- [POLICY] Title maximum 30 chars, short description maximum 80, full description maximum 4,000. Avoid
  irrelevant repeated keywords, impersonation and unsupported superlatives. Source: Google Play listing
  guides below.
- [POLICY] Since Aug 31, 2026, standard new Android apps and updates must target Android 16 (API 36) or
  newer, with exceptions for some form factors; currently targetSdk 37, but compatibility must be
  demonstrated.
- [POLICY] For new personal developer accounts opened after Nov 13, 2023, at least 12 testers must remain
  opted in to closed testing continuously for 14 days before applying for production access;
  applicability depends on developer account.
- [POLICY] Publicly accessible privacy policy on website AND within app, even if no data collected. A PDF
  alone is not an acceptable privacy URL.
- [POLICY] Data safety declaration is required for tracks generally, including SDK behavior; on-device-
  only information is generally not off-device collection. Confirm real behavior before answering.
- [POLICY] Financial features declaration required on Play for published apps including testing tracks;
  accurately choose the type(s) that fit actual features and disclosure instructions.
- [POLICY] Play image requirements: 512px PNG icon, feature graphic 1024x500 JPEG/24-bit PNG, genuine
  compliant screenshots. See official policy URLs below.
- [RELEASE] Developer contact/support email, policy pages, appropriate content rating, signing key,
  stable rollout/review response plan and region/language configuration must be finalized in Play
  Console.


====================================================================================================
15 | PRIVACY POLICY, SUPPORT FAQ AND TRUST COPY NEEDED
====================================================================================================

- Privacy policy draft outline: identity/contact and last updated; data user enters (salary, category
  plans, transactions, preferences); purpose of processing; exact local storage location; third-party SDK
  inventory and data transfers if any; export/sharing behavior; retention/deletion; security measures
  actually used; region/country rights and contact.
- Core privacy promise (conditional): 'You can create a budget without connecting a bank account.' Do not
  claim 'zero data collected' before dependency and analytics audit.
- Support FAQ: 'What is Plan vs Actual?' Plan is intended allocation; Actual is recorded activity. 'Is
  bank sync required?' No for core manual workflow. 'Where is data stored?' Locally, pending verified
  release audit. 'Can I change phones?' Confirm only after full backup restore launches.
- Help center topics: quick start, currencies/rounding, monthly close, category presets, backup &
  restore, PDF/CSV export, missing income, troubleshooting/bug report and app version.
- Feedback email format: app version, Android version, reproduce steps, whether backup exists; never
  request passwords, banking OTPs or private salary documents.
- Clear data warning: deleting app may lose locally stored records when backup/restore is absent. Explain
  frankly in onboarding and FAQ until solved.
- Plain financial disclaimer: this app records budgets and is not a bank, lender, credit advisor or
  source of guaranteed financial outcomes. Avoid fake percentile health claims.


====================================================================================================
16 | PRICING, ANALYTICS, GROWTH AND UNIT ECONOMICS
====================================================================================================

- Present state: no subscription/billing operation was verified from audited repo; proposed models below
  are NOT features already built.
- Launch hypothesis: core manual budgets, transaction history, full backup & restore, CSV and essential
  reports free to establish trust; test pricing later with actual users.
- Optional one-time upgrade: premium report templates and visual personalization, not essential data
  recovery. If family cloud sync adds recurring costs, test fair subscription separately.
- Avoid disruptive ads over sensitive balances or critical logging flows. Avoid changing a previously
  sold lifetime entitlement without a robust restore path.
- Illustration only: 1,000 visitors x 20% install conversion = 200 downloads; 200 x 30% D30 retention =
  60 retained; 60 x 3% payer conversion = ~2 payers. These values are assumptions, NOT forecasts,
  benchmarks or observed revenue.
- North-star metric proposed: users who finish a real monthly salary plan AND record >=3 transactions in
  that month. It requires privacy-respecting measurement.
- Supporting metrics: listing conversion, install-to-first-plan, time to first expense, D1/D7/D30
  retention, backup completed, restore success, export correctness, crash-free sessions, user reports and
  refunds.
- Community acquisition: authentic salary budgeting tutorials, small beta cohorts, helpful India-specific
  examples, organic social demos, user permission for community sharing and a privacy-respecting
  informational website.
- ASO experiments: test salary-first copy against household-first copy with constant images; use Play's
  controlled experiments, sufficient sample, verify both conversion and meaningful app activation. Do not
  manipulate ratings or buy fake reviews.

17 | 90-DAY EXECUTION CALENDAR AND ACCEPTANCE GATES
====================================================================================================

Day 0-3 P0 | Fix two Kotlin compilation call-site/signature errors, remove unsupported claims and production seeding.
   Owner: Android engineering. Exit gate: assembleDebug, testDebugUnitTest, lintDebug pass; clean install empty.

Day 4-10 P0 | Reconcile plan vs actual financial formulas; test totals, no-income and exports.
   Owner: Android/data. Exit gate: No mismatch to paise across UI and reports.

Day 8-21 P0 | Build versioned all-history export/import restore, validation, duplicate policy and safe database transaction.
   Owner: Android/data and QA. Exit gate: Fresh install restore retains all dates, categories, accounts, transactions and totals.

Day 15-30 P1 | Make onboarding real, responsive summary-first dashboard, fast transaction entry and accessibility changes.
   Owner: UX + engineering. Exit gate: 10-person usability trial: <=2min first budget, no ambiguous sample data.

Day 20-35 P1 | Device matrix, process death, database migration, large data and low storage validation.
   Owner: QA. Exit gate: All high-risk cases pass; critical defect count zero.

Day 30-45 P1 | Final app identity, package/brand clearance, support/privacy site and policy form responses.
   Owner: Play publisher. Exit gate: All Play Console declarations truthful for signed artifact.

Day 40-60 P1 | Closed testing with required testers where applicable, bug triage, accessibility follow-up.
   Owner: Publisher + beta. Exit gate: No unresolved data loss or wrong-money cases.

Day 60-75 P2 | Build final screenshot/video assets, listing experiments, Hindi copy only after QA.
   Owner: Design + ASO. Exit gate: No false product promise; all art passes Play specs.

Day 75-90 P2 | Staged production only after gates; monitor activation, retention, crash and review reports.
   Owner: Release owner. Exit gate: Healthy cohort and rollback/incident plan documented.

After Day 90 P3 | Recurring bills, trend reports, optional family sync and advanced features using measured feedback.
   Owner: Product. Exit gate: Data portability and prior UX remain intact.

Indicative planning effort only, not a promise: build/correctness 1-2 weeks, full import/restore 2-4
weeks, UX/accessibility 2-4 weeks, QA and Play operations 2-4 weeks, with overlap based on team
availability.

18 | EXPLICIT TEST MATRIX FOR RELEASE ACCEPTANCE
====================================================================================================

01. Fresh install never shows Rs 70,000 fictional income as the user's real income.
02. No transactions/no income -> zero actual income and clearly labeled planned values.
03. One income, one expense, one saving, one investment and refund are correctly classified.
04. Categories sum correctly with zero values, negative remainder and large values.
05. January/December transitions and timezone changes do not move financial records into wrong month.
06. All minor-unit amounts keep INR paise, no floating rounding or Long overflow.
07. Actual vs plan toggles preserve distinct income and remaining-value semantics.
08. Room totals equal Home, CSV, PDF and PNG numbers for same month exactly.
09. Complete backup and restore into empty app produces identical row IDs, plans, accounts and totals.
10. Restore corrupt/truncated/newer schema refuses safely without destroying current data.
11. Repeat restore/merge does not double-count entries; duplicates handled explicitly.
12. Remove/archive/recover categories preserves historical transactions and reports.
13. Rapid month switches, concurrent edits, duplicate taps and process recreation are safe.
14. Storage full/export canceled: errors explained, no phantom success or inaccessible file.
15. The app works on supported Android 8/13/16 and newest target-capable test device.
16. Text 200%, long category names, 320dp width, system dark mode and landscape layout readable.
17. TalkBack reads amounts, screen order, warnings and charts with non-color text alternatives.
18. All interactive targets large enough and keyboard does not cover Save action.
19. 1000 and 10000 transaction fixture: scrolling, startup and reporting measured.
20. App upgrade from prior DB schema retains user data.
21. FileProvider shares only expected content with read grants and cleans cache safely.
22. Privacy/permissions auditing matches final merged manifest and SDKs.
23. Signed AAB installation through Play internal track works, not only debug APK.
24. Store listing, feature graphic, app icon, screenshots and privacy link match actual shipped features.
25. Release notes, support email, Financial features declaration and Data safety submitted accurately.
26. No unsupported health percentile or competitor accusation remains in production.
27. Beta users finish first plan without coaching and understand when figures are samples.
28. Loss/recovery rehearsal documents what to do when changing phones.
29. Any paywall or paid unlock, if added, can restore valid purchase on a new device.
30. All P0 issues signed off before production rollout.
